{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"Phase 2: Constrained extraction with grammar (XGrammar) + semantic (TypeConstraintProcessor) layers.\\n\\nModes:\\n  unconstrained  - no constraints (baseline)\\n  grammar_only   - XGrammar JSON/enum structure enforcement\\n  semantic_only  - TypeConstraintProcessor domain/range masking\\n  full_s1        - both layers, single-pass\\n  full_s2        - two-pass: entities first, then constrained relations\\n\\nDatasets: scierc (sentence-level), docred (document-level)\\nOutput: JSONL compatible with violation_checker.py\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport os\\nimport re\\nimport time\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nimport torch\\nfrom transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer\\n\\nfrom compiler.ontology_compiler import ConstraintSpec, RelationConstraint\\nfrom compiler.type_mask_processor import TypeConstraintProcessor\\n\\n\\ndef _chat_template_kwargs(tokenizer):\\n    name = getattr(tokenizer, 'name_or_path', '').lower()\\n    if 'qwen' in name:\\n        return {'enable_thinking': False}\\n    return {}\\n\\n\\nRAW_TO_CANONICAL = {\\n    \\\"USED-FOR\\\": \\\"Used-for\\\", \\\"FEATURE-OF\\\": \\\"Feature-of\\\",\\n    \\\"HYPONYM-OF\\\": \\\"Hyponym-of\\\", \\\"PART-OF\\\": \\\"Part-of\\\",\\n    \\\"COMPARE\\\": \\\"Compare\\\", \\\"CONJUNCTION\\\": \\\"Conjunction\\\",\\n    \\\"EVALUATE-FOR\\\": \\\"Evaluate-for\\\",\\n}\\n\\n# DocRED entity type order must match Phase 0 (run_extraction_docred.py)\\nDOCRED_ENTITY_TYPES = [\\\"PER\\\", \\\"ORG\\\", \\\"LOC\\\", \\\"TIME\\\", \\\"NUM\\\", \\\"MISC\\\"]\\n\\nSCIERC_SYSTEM = \\\"\\\"\\\"You are an expert information extraction system for scientific texts. Extract entities and relations from the given text.\\n\\nEntity types: Task, Method, Material, Metric, OtherScientificTerm, Generic\\nRelation types: Used-for, Feature-of, Hyponym-of, Part-of, Compare, Conjunction, Evaluate-for\\n\\nOutput a JSON object with:\\n- \\\"entities\\\": list of {\\\"text\\\": \\\"...\\\", \\\"type\\\": \\\"...\\\"}\\n- \\\"relations\\\": list of {\\\"head\\\": \\\"...\\\", \\\"tail\\\": \\\"...\\\", \\\"type\\\": \\\"...\\\", \\\"head_type\\\": \\\"...\\\", \\\"tail_type\\\": \\\"...\\\"}\\n\\nOutput ONLY valid JSON, no explanation.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nSCIERC_USER_TEMPLATE = \\\"\\\"\\\"Extract all entities and relations from this scientific text:\\n\\n\\\"{text}\\\"\\n\\nOutput JSON:\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n\\n# -- Data Loading --\\n\\ndef load_scierc_data(data_path):\\n    samples = []\\n    with open(data_path) as f:\\n        for line in f:\\n            line = line.strip()\\n            if not line:\\n                continue\\n            doc = json.loads(line)\\n            doc_id = doc.get(\\\"doc_key\\\", \\\"unknown\\\")\\n            sentences = doc.get(\\\"sentences\\\", [])\\n            ner_all = doc.get(\\\"ner\\\", [])\\n            rel_all = doc.get(\\\"relations\\\", [])\\n\\n            offsets = []\\n            cum = 0\\n            for s in sentences:\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.43}}"}

    "structuredContent": {
      "result": "{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"Phase 2: Constrained extraction with grammar (XGrammar) + semantic (TypeConstraintProcessor) layers.\\n\\nModes:\\n  unconstrained  - no constraints (baseline)\\n  grammar_only   - XGrammar JSON/enum structure enforcement\\n  semantic_only  - TypeConstraintProcessor domain/range masking\\n  full_s1        - both layers, single-pass\\n  full_s2        - two-pass: entities first, then constrained relations\\n\\nDatasets: scierc (sentence-level), docred (document-level)\\nOutput: JSONL compatible with violation_checker.py\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport os\\nimport re\\nimport time\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nimport torch\\nfrom transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer\\n\\nfrom compiler.ontology_compiler import ConstraintSpec, RelationConstraint\\nfrom compiler.type_mask_processor import TypeConstraintProcessor\\n\\n\\ndef _chat_template_kwargs(tokenizer):\\n    name = getattr(tokenizer, 'name_or_path', '').lower()\\n    if 'qwen' in name:\\n        return {'enable_thinking': False}\\n    return {}\\n\\n\\nRAW_TO_CANONICAL = {\\n    \\\"USED-FOR\\\": \\\"Used-for\\\", \\\"FEATURE-OF\\\": \\\"Feature-of\\\",\\n    \\\"HYPONYM-OF\\\": \\\"Hyponym-of\\\", \\\"PART-OF\\\": \\\"Part-of\\\",\\n    \\\"COMPARE\\\": \\\"Compare\\\", \\\"CONJUNCTION\\\": \\\"Conjunction\\\",\\n    \\\"EVALUATE-FOR\\\": \\\"Evaluate-for\\\",\\n}\\n\\n# DocRED entity type order must match Phase 0 (run_extraction_docred.py)\\nDOCRED_ENTITY_TYPES = [\\\"PER\\\", \\\"ORG\\\", \\\"LOC\\\", \\\"TIME\\\", \\\"NUM\\\", \\\"MISC\\\"]\\n\\nSCIERC_SYSTEM = \\\"\\\"\\\"You are an expert information extraction system for scientific texts. Extract entities and relations from the given text.\\n\\nEntity types: Task, Method, Material, Metric, OtherScientificTerm, Generic\\nRelation types: Used-for, Feature-of, Hyponym-of, Part-of, Compare, Conjunction, Evaluate-for\\n\\nOutput a JSON object with:\\n- \\\"entities\\\": list of {\\\"text\\\": \\\"...\\\", \\\"type\\\": \\\"...\\\"}\\n- \\\"relations\\\": list of {\\\"head\\\": \\\"...\\\", \\\"tail\\\": \\\"...\\\", \\\"type\\\": \\\"...\\\", \\\"head_type\\\": \\\"...\\\", \\\"tail_type\\\": \\\"...\\\"}\\n\\nOutput ONLY valid JSON, no explanation.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nSCIERC_USER_TEMPLATE = \\\"\\\"\\\"Extract all entities and relations from this scientific text:\\n\\n\\\"{text}\\\"\\n\\nOutput JSON:\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n\\n# -- Data Loading --\\n\\ndef load_scierc_data(data_path):\\n    samples = []\\n    with open(data_path) as f:\\n        for line in f:\\n            line = line.strip()\\n            if not line:\\n                continue\\n            doc = json.loads(line)\\n            doc_id = doc.get(\\\"doc_key\\\", \\\"unknown\\\")\\n            sentences = doc.get(\\\"sentences\\\", [])\\n            ner_all = doc.get(\\\"ner\\\", [])\\n            rel_all = doc.get(\\\"relations\\\", [])\\n\\n            offsets = []\\n            cum = 0\\n            for s in sentences:\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.43}}"

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"Phase 2: Constrained extraction with grammar (XGrammar) + semantic (TypeConstraintProcessor) layers.\\n\\nModes:\\n  unconstrained  - no constraints (baseline)\\n  grammar_only   - XGrammar JSON/enum structure enforcement\\n  semantic_only  - TypeConstraintProcessor domain/range masking\\n  full_s1        - both layers, single-pass\\n  full_s2        - two-pass: entities first, then constrained relations\\n\\nDatasets: scierc (sentence-level), docred (document-level)\\nOutput: JSONL compatible with violation_checker.py\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport os\\nimport re\\nimport time\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nimport torch\\nfrom transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer\\n\\nfrom compiler.ontology_compiler import ConstraintSpec, RelationConstraint\\nfrom compiler.type_mask_processor import TypeConstraintProcessor\\n\\n\\ndef _chat_template_kwargs(tokenizer):\\n    name = getattr(tokenizer, 'name_or_path', '').lower()\\n    if 'qwen' in name:\\n        return {'enable_thinking': False}\\n    return {}\\n\\n\\nRAW_TO_CANONICAL = {\\n    \\\"USED-FOR\\\": \\\"Used-for\\\", \\\"FEATURE-OF\\\": \\\"Feature-of\\\",\\n    \\\"HYPONYM-OF\\\": \\\"Hyponym-of\\\", \\\"PART-OF\\\": \\\"Part-of\\\",\\n    \\\"COMPARE\\\": \\\"Compare\\\", \\\"CONJUNCTION\\\": \\\"Conjunction\\\",\\n    \\\"EVALUATE-FOR\\\": \\\"Evaluate-for\\\",\\n}\\n\\n# DocRED entity type order must match Phase 0 (run_extraction_docred.py)\\nDOCRED_ENTITY_TYPES = [\\\"PER\\\", \\\"ORG\\\", \\\"LOC\\\", \\\"TIME\\\", \\\"NUM\\\", \\\"MISC\\\"]\\n\\nSCIERC_SYSTEM = \\\"\\\"\\\"You are an expert information extraction system for scientific texts. Extract entities and relations from the given text.\\n\\nEntity types: Task, Method, Material, Metric, OtherScientificTerm, Generic\\nRelation types: Used-for, Feature-of, Hyponym-of, Part-of, Compare, Conjunction, Evaluate-for\\n\\nOutput a JSON object with:\\n- \\\"entities\\\": list of {\\\"text\\\": \\\"...\\\", \\\"type\\\": \\\"...\\\"}\\n- \\\"relations\\\": list of {\\\"head\\\": \\\"...\\\", \\\"tail\\\": \\\"...\\\", \\\"type\\\": \\\"...\\\", \\\"head_type\\\": \\\"...\\\", \\\"tail_type\\\": \\\"...\\\"}\\n\\nOutput ONLY valid JSON, no explanation.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nSCIERC_USER_TEMPLATE = \\\"\\\"\\\"Extract all entities and relations from this scientific text:\\n\\n\\\"{text}\\\"\\n\\nOutput JSON:\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\n\\n# -- Data Loading --\\n\\ndef load_scierc_data(data_path):\\n    samples = []\\n    with open(data_path) as f:\\n        for line in f:\\n            line = line.strip()\\n            if not line:\\n                continue\\n            doc = json.loads(line)\\n            doc_id = doc.get(\\\"doc_key\\\", \\\"unknown\\\")\\n            sentences = doc.get(\\\"sentences\\\", [])\\n            ner_all = doc.get(\\\"ner\\\", [])\\n            rel_all = doc.get(\\\"relations\\\", [])\\n\\n            offsets = []\\n            cum = 0\\n            for s in sentences:\\n                offsets.append(cum)\\n                cum += len(s)\\n\\n            for sent_idx, tokens in enumerate(sentences):\\n                sentence_text = \\\" \\\".join(tokens)\\n                offset = offsets[sent_idx]\\n\\n                gold_entities = []\\n                if sent_idx < len(ner_all):\\n                    for start, end, etype in ner_all[sent_idx]:\\n                        mention = \\\" \\\".join(tokens[start - offset:end - offset + 1])\\n                        gold_entities.append({\\\"text\\\": mention, \\\"type\\\": etype})\\n\\n                gold_relations = []\\n                if sent_idx < len(rel_all):\\n                    span_type = {}\\n                    if sent_idx < len(ner_all):\\n                        for s, e, et in ner_all[sent_idx]:\\n                            span_type[(s, e)] = et\\n                    for h_s, h_e, t_s, t_e, rtype_raw in rel_all[sent_idx]:\\n                        head_text = \\\" \\\".join(tokens[h_s - offset:h_e - offset + 1])\\n                        tail_text = \\\" \\\".join(tokens[t_s - offset:t_e - offset + 1])\\n                        head_type = span_type.get((h_s, h_e), \\\"UNKNOWN\\\")\\n                        tail_type = span_type.get((t_s, t_e), \\\"UNKNOWN\\\")\\n                        rtype = RAW_TO_CANONICAL.get(rtype_raw, rtype_raw)\\n                        gold_relations.append({\\n                            \\\"head\\\": head_text, \\\"tail\\\": tail_text, \\\"type\\\": rtype,\\n                            \\\"head_type\\\": head_type, \\\"tail_type\\\": tail_type,\\n \\n\\n... s[\\\"doc_id\\\"] not in processed_ids]\\n    skipped = len(samples) - len(todo)\\n    if args.resume and skipped > 0:\\n        print(f\\\"Resuming from {skipped} processed samples, {len(todo)} remaining\\\")\\n\\n    file_mode = \\\"a\\\" if args.resume else \\\"w\\\"\\n    n_ok, n_fail = 0, 0\\n\\n    with open(args.output_path, file_mode) as fout:\\n        for idx, (i, sample) in enumerate(todo):\\n            t0 = time.time()\\n\\n            if args.mode == \\\"full_s2\\\":\\n                raw, parsed = run_s2_two_pass(\\n                    model, tokenizer, sample, 脚本已就绪。汇总：

**脚本路径**: `scripts/run_docred_lora_eval.sh`

**Merged checkpoint 路径**:
- Qwen: `/root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints/qwen_lora_docred_merged`
- Llama: `/root/autodl-tmp/checkpoints/llama_lora_docred_merged`

**4 个 eval 命令**:

| 命令 | mode | model | device | output |
|------|------|-------|--------|--------|
| `qwen_unconstrained` | unconstrained | qwen_lora_docred_merged | cuda:1 | `outputs/w5_qwen_docred_lora_unconstrained_eval.jsonl` |
| `qwen_constrained` | full_s1 | qwen_lora_docred_merged | cuda:1 | `outputs/w5_qwen_docred_lora_constrained_eval.jsonl` |
| `llama_unconstrained` | unconstrained | llama_lora_docred_merged | cuda:0 | `outputs/w5_llama_docred_lora_unconstrained_eval.jsonl` |
| `llama_constrained` | full_s1 | llama_lora_docred_merged | cuda:0 | `outputs/w5_llama_docred_lora_constrained_eval.jsonl` |

所有命令：`--dataset docred`, `--data_path data/docred/dev.json`, `--constraints_path data/docred/ontology_constraints_docred.json`, `--max_new_tokens 4096`, `--batch_size 1`

**DocRED vs SciERC 差异**:
- 无特殊处理差异。`run_constrained_extraction.py` 通过 `--dataset docred` 参数切换数据加载函数 (`load_docred_data`) 和 prompt 模板，约束文件也是独立的 `ontology_constraints_docred.json`
- SciERC 的 merged model 在 `checkpoints/` 下有 symlink → autodl-tmp；DocRED 训练完成后需要确认 merged 目录是否正确写入（或手动创建 symlink）

**用法**: `bash scripts/run_docred_lora_eval.sh qwen_unconstrained` 单跑，或 `all` 全跑。语法校验通过。
